Previous used exact CD. Potential weaknesses:
- SLSQP smooth max with logsum may leave kinks; exact CD fixed/random.
- Subgradient and pair swap crude.
- Multi-n interpolation maybe not enough.
- No enforcement symmetry or low-dimensional global.
- Project_feasible by proportional scaling can break local structure.

Generic:
```
def build_coeff(h, idxs, M, off):
  L=np.zeros(M); Q=np.zeros(M,int)
  s=len(idxs)
  for u in idxs:
    # u as h index, v all not in S
    mask = np.ones(n,bool); mask[idxs]=False
    rmask = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
    L[off + u - rmask] += (1-h[rmask])
    # v as 1-h index, u all not in S
    L[off + rmask - u] += -h[rmask]
  for u in idxs:
    for v in idxs:
      Q[off+u-v]+=1
  return L,Q
```
But rmask excludes all idxs. For pair, loop u=p then q, each adds contributions with outside r. For offdiag u=p,v=q, excluded from L and Q handles. Good.
Index arrays: off+u-vmask may be length n-s; numpy boolean indexing? Use integer indices from np.arange(n)[mask]. Vectorizable.
However, for single CD n=700, building L each coordinate with boolean masks O(n) and arrays allocations. Fine. For 700 coordinates/pass, 700*700 ~490k operations per pass plus allocations maybe okay. Could precompute base index arrays for each p? off+p - r and off+r-p. But h values vary. We can compute with vectorized slices:

project_feasible: similar to previous but perhaps faster.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
    target=n/2
    for _ in range(200):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum(); if tot<1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h+(diff/tot)*room,0,1)
       else:
          tot=h.sum(); if tot<1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h+(diff/tot)*h,0,1)
    # exact adjust by distributing residual among fractional elements
    # To make sum exactly target, after clipping still diff maybe. We can adjust a subset.
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>0:
       if diff>0:
          # find room positive
          room=(1-h)>1e-15
          # need exact; sort? We can add to first room proportional with clipping.
       else: similar
    return h
```

build_coeff:
```
def build_coeff(h, idxs, n=None):
   n=len(h); M=2*n-1; off=n-1
   L=np.zeros(M)
   idx=np.arange(n)
   for u in idxs:
      L[off + u - idx] += 1.0 - h[idx]
      L[off + idx - u] += -h[idx]
   Q=np.zeros(M, dtype=np.int8)
   for u in idxs:
      for v in idxs:
         t=off+u-v
         L[t] -= 1.0 - h[v]
         L[off+v-u] += h[v]
         Q[t] += 1
   return L,Q
```

Feasibility function:
```
def feasible_pair(c, L, Q, a, gamma):
    xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0
    pos = L > 1e-14
    if np.any(pos):
       ub = (gamma - c[pos]) / L[pos]
       xhi = min(xhi, float(np.max(ub)))? Wait need min over all upper bounds. For L>0, x ≤ (γ-c)/L. So xhi = min(xhi, min(ub)). Not max. I earlier said min. yes.
    neg = L < -1e-14
    if np.any(neg):
       lb = (gamma - c[neg]) / L[neg]  # L negative
       xlo = max(xlo, float(np.max(lb)))? Let's test L=-1, constraint -x≤γ. c=0: (γ)/-1 = -γ. Need x≥-γ. If multiple, x≥max values. Since values may be negative. Use max.
    # L=0: if c[zero] > gamma + tol: infeasible
    if xlo > xhi + tol: return False, [(xlo,xhi)]
    intervals = [(max(0,xlo), min(1,xhi))]
    # quadratic forbidden
    for t in qidx:
       Qv=Q[t]
       B=L[t]+Qv
       C=c[t] - L[t]*a - Qv*a*(1-a)
       # P(x)= Q x^2 - B x + (gamma-C) >=0
       disc = B*B - 4*Qv*(gamma-C)
       if disc <= 0:
           if -disc < 1e-14? If disc <0 always feasible; if =0 touches point, constraint fails only at one point? P>=0 all except root? At root zero, feasible. If disc=0, P>=0 (double root) all x; no forbidden interval. if disc<0 feasible. So only disc>tol -> interval.
       else:
           sqrtD=np.sqrt(disc)
           r1=(-B - sqrtD)/(2*Qv)
           r2=(-B + sqrtD)/(2*Qv)
           f1=max(0.0,r1); f2=min(1.0,r2)
           if f1 < f2:
              split intervals; if empty return False
    return True, intervals
```

```

CD function:
```
def coordinate_descent(h, n, mode, passes, deadline, rng):
    # h should feasible; recompute c
    c = np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full')
    cmax = c.max()*2/n
    for p in range(passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       if mode=='sym':
          h=0.5*(h+h[::-1]) # maybe ensure pairs; but recompute c? Already symmetric; no need each pass if updates preserve. Could do once.
          groups = n//2
          order = rng.permutation(n//2)
          for gi in order:
              p=gi; q=n-1-p
              x,L,Q=...; update
       else:
          groups=n; order=rng.permutation(n)
          for p in range(n): idxs=(p,)
       c = np.correlate(...)
       newcmax=...
       if newcmax >= cmax - 1e-11: maybe stop after few? We can break if no improvement over pass.
       cmax=newcmax
    return h,cmax*2/n
```
But if we recompute c at end of pass, cmax count? Return bounding C.

def run(seed,budget_s):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 best_h=None; best_c=1.0; best_n=0
 # parent
 try: h=np.array(initial_h_values,float); n=len(h)
 except: n=500; h=generate_initial(...'smooth')
 if n%2: n=n//2? We need even. If parent odd? likely 700. Ensure even: if n odd, trim to n-1 and project.
 h=project_feasible(h,n)
 update_best(h,n)
 # sym parent
 hs=sym_feasible(h)
 update
 # CD on full parent? maybe 30s
 if time<deadline-20: hf=cd(hs? or full, mode full, stop=deadline-?); update
 # sym CD best sym for longer
 hs=cd(hs, sym, stop=deadline-100); update
 # full CD from hs short
 hf=cd(hs, full, stop=deadline-50); update
 # multi-n interpolation: nlist [200,300,400,500,600]
 best currently; for each n_try not equal, interp from best_h to n_try, sym_feasible, cd sym with allocated time, update; if better, continue with new best_n.
 # Fourier low-dim search from scratch for small n and larger n
 if time<deadline-200:
    for nF in [350, 512?]: generate h from fourier_opt(nF, R=4, time limit)
    update and cd.
 # noise restarts: for sigma list, perturb best sym, cd short, update
 # final polish best with mode sym/full until deadline-3
 # final project/sym? If best unsym full, project. If sym, sym.
 return best_h, best_c, best_n

def solve_set(c, h, idxs):
    a = h[idxs[0]]
    L,Q = build_coeff(h, idxs, len(h))
    cmax = float(c.max())
    # If a already feasible with cmax? yes.
    # Lower bound: maybe max of baseline at x=a? no.
    lo = 0.0
    hi = cmax
    # To reduce iterations, if cmax==0 return a.
    pos = L > 1e-12; neg = L < -1e-12; zero = ~(pos|neg)
    zero_c = c[zero] if any
    qidx = np.flatnonzero(Q > 0)
    # Precompute for each qidx? B, C fixed independent of gamma? C includes -Q*a(1-a). disc depends gamma.
    qinfo=[]
    for t in qidx:
       q=Q[t]; b=L[t]+q; cc=c[t] - L[t]*a - q*a*(1-a)
       qinfo.append((q,b,cc))
    for _ in range(45):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       if feasible_mid(mid,...): hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    ok,intervals=feasible_final(hi)
    if not ok: return a
    x=choose_closest(intervals,a)
    return x,L,Q,a
```

```
def check_intervals(gamma, c, L, pos_mask, neg_mask, zero_mask, qinfo):
   if np.any(zero_mask) and c[zero_mask].max() > gamma + 1e-12: return False, None
   xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0
   if pos_mask.any():
      # c[pos] & L[pos]
      ub=(gamma - c[pos_mask]) / L[pos_mask]
      xhi = min(xhi, float(np.min(ub)))
   if neg_mask.any():
      lb=(gamma - c[neg_mask]) / L[neg_mask]
      xlo = max(xlo, float(np.max(lb)))
   xlo = max(0.0, xlo); xhi = min(1.0, xhi)
   if xlo > xhi + 1e-12: return False,None
   intervals=[(xlo,xhi)]
   for q,b,cc in qinfo:
       disc=b*b - 4.0*q*(gamma-cc)
       if disc > 1e-12:
          sd=np.sqrt(disc)
          r1=(-b-sd)/(2.0*q); r2=(-b+sd)/(2.0*q)
          f1=max(0.0,r1); f2=min(1.0,r2)
          if f1 < f2:
             new=[]
             for s,e in intervals:
                if e <= f1 or s >= f2: new.append((s,e))
                else:
                   if s < f1: new.append((s,f1))
                   if f2 < e: new.append((f2,e))
             if not new: return False,None
             intervals=new
   # Remove tiny/invalid
   intervals=[(max(0.0,s),min(1.0,e)) for s,e in intervals if e-s>1e-14]
   if not intervals: return False,None
   return True,intervals
```
Tolerance: If disc small positive, root interval tiny; forbidden negligible. Use >1e-10 maybe. But if interval excludes only point, no issue; if tiny gap and feasible outside, we can ignore? To be safe, if disc > 1e-10. If disc <=0 feasible. For exactly tangent, feasible all with one equality, okay. Good.

Note c[zero_mask].max() each γ O(n). Could precompute zero_max? Since c fixed per set, yes zero_max = c[zero_mask].max() if any. Then check gamma >= zero_max. Since constraints with L=0 independent of x. This also gives lower bound. In bisection, if gamma < zero_max, infeasible. We can set lo=max(lo, zero_max). Feasibility just if gamma < zero_max return false. Good.
For pos/neg, if xlo/xhi after division outside, etc.
Also, if pos_mask empty, no upper. If neg empty, no lower.
Formats: qinfo list of tuples. Build once.

Let's refine coordinate_descent for time and pass stopping:
```
def coordinate_descent(h, n, mode, stop_until, rng, sym=True):
    deadline=stop_until
    h=np.array(h,float)
    if mode=='sym':
       h=project_feasible_sym(h)
    else:
       h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
    bound = c.max()*2/n
    # Keep best passing maybe; if projection increases, we can revert h to best? We update h in place; at end return best.
    best_h=h.copy(); best_bound=bound; best_c=c.copy()?
    no_improve=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if mode=='sym':
          h=0.5*(h+h[::-1]); # ensure
          # if not sym? okay
          order=rng.permutation(n//2)
          for gi in order:
             if time.time()>deadline: break
             p=gi; q=n-1-p
             x,L,Q,a=... solve
             update
       else:
          order=rng.permutation(n)
          for p in order: ...
       # end pass: project
       if mode=='sym': h=sym_feasible(h)
       else: h=project_feasible(h,n)
       c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
       bound=c.max()*2/n
       if bound < best_bound - 1e-12:
          best_bound=bound; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve>=5: break
    if h bound better? best tracked.
    return best_h,best_bound
```
Need if CD using unprojected h within pass but best only after projection. If projection worsens, best_h from previous. Good.

But within pass, we don't project; after pass, if bound worsens, best_h remains previous. The next pass starts from h projected (possibly worse than best_h). Should we reset h to best_h if projection worsened? Good idea:
```
if bound > best_bound + 1e-8:
    h = best_h.copy()
    c = correlate(...)
```
This avoids drifting into worse basin. But if current h after projection worse, starting best may be better. We'll do: after pass, if bound < best_bound - 1e-10: update best, keep h; else if bound > best_bound + 1e-9: h=best_h.copy(); c=...; no_improve++; if no_improve > some break.
This makes CD quasi-monotone.

However, if within pass we make small improvements but final projection worsens slightly, resetting may lose. But best previous saved. Good.

Need c for next pass if reset: recompute.

def coeff_single(h,p,n):
 M=2*n-1; off=n-1; idx=np.arange(n) # if pass idx precomputed? Could use global? Not global. Compute each? np.arange(n) 700*700? okay but allocate each. Better have idx in CD and pass.

Use functions with `idx` argument to avoid arange:
```
def coeff_single(h,p,idx,M,off):
 L=np.zeros(M)
 L[off+p-idx] += 1.0 - h[idx]
 L[off+idx-p] -= h[idx]
 t=off

 Q=(np.zeros(M)); Q[off]=1
 return L,Q
```

```

# p,p

# q,q
L[off] -= 1-b; L[off] += b; Q[off]+=1
# p,q

# q,p
L[off+q-p] -= 1-a; L[off+p-q] += a; Q[off+q-p]+=1
```

def solve_x(c,L,Q,a):
   cmax=float(c.max())
   if cmax <= 0: return a
   pos=L>1e-12; neg=L<-1e-12; zero=~(pos|neg)
   zero_bound = float(c[zero].max()) if zero.any() else 0.0
   lo=max(0.0, zero_bound)
   hi=cmax
   qinfo=[]
   qidx=np.nonzero(Q>0)[0]
   for t in qidx:
      q=Q[t]; b=L[t]+q; cc=c[t] - L[t]*a - q*a*(1.0-a)
      qinfo.append((q,b,cc))
   # If hi not feasible due numerical? try a? It should. If check(hi) false, hi=cmax+? maybe use x=a fallback.
   for _ in range(40):
      mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
      ok,_=check(mid,..)
      if ok: hi=mid
      else: lo=mid
   ok,intervals=check(hi,...)
   if not ok:
      # if hi too low due numerical, try cmax
      ok,intervals=check(cmax,...)
      if not ok: return a
   return choose_closest(intervals,a)
```

```
def project_sym(h):
    h=np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
    n=len(h)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    h=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # Now adjust pairs to sum
    target=n*0.5
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # distribute to pairs with available space/mass
       first=np.arange(n//2)
       for _ in range(50):
          diff=target-h.sum()
          if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
          if diff>0:
             vals=0.5*(h[first]+h[n-1-first])
             room=(1.0-vals)
             mask=room>1e-12
             cnt=mask.sum()
             if cnt==0: break
             add=diff/(2.0*cnt)
             h[first[mask]] += add; h[n-1-first[mask]] += add
             h=np.clip(h,0,1)
          else:
             vals=...
             mask=vals>1e-12
             remove=diff/(2.0*cnt) # negative
             h[first[mask]] += remove; h[n-1-first[mask]] += remove
             h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
This preserves symmetry and sum better. But O(50*n) small. We can just call project_feasible then sym; pair sum enough? We'll include pair adjust for symmetric.

Let's implement project_feasible carefully:
```
def project_feasible(h,n=None):
    if n is None: n=len(h)
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(200):
        s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h=np.clip(h+(diff/tot)*room,0.0,1.0)
        else:
            tot=s
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h=np.clip(h+(diff/tot)*h,0.0,1.0)
    # exact tail adjust
    s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
        if diff>0:
            idxs=np.where(h<1.0)[0]
        else:
            idxs=np.where(h>0.0)[0]
        if len(idxs):
            h[idxs[0]] += diff  # but must keep bounds
    if h[0]<0? Actually diff small and idx safe. To keep bounds, distribute:
       add=diff/len(idxs); h[idxs]+=add; clip; if residual adjust one.
```
Simpler: after loop diff usually <1e-12. Fine. Use `if h[idxs[0]]+diff within bounds`. But no need exact. We'll implement robust:
```
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>0:
       if diff>0:
          idxs=np.where(h<1.0-1e-15)[0]
       else: idxs=np.where(h>1e-15)[0]
       if len(idxs):
            add=diff/min(len(idxs), 100)
            h[idxs]=np.clip(h[idxs]+add,0,1)
            diff2=target-h.sum()
            if abs(diff2)>0:
               # adjust one idx with room/mass
               j=idxs[0]
               h[j]=np.clip(h[j]+diff2,0,1)
```

def project_sym(h):
    n=len(h)
    h=np.clip(np.array(h,float),0,1)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    h=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
    # pair adjust for sum
    target=n/2.0
    first=np.arange(n//2)
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       vals=0.5*(h[first]+h[n-1-first])
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-vals
          mask=room>1e-14
          if not mask.any(): break
          add=diff/(2.0*mask.sum())
          # if add>room for some, clip then loop
          h[first[mask]]+=add; h[n-1-first[mask]]+=add
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       else:
          mass=vals
          mask=mass>1e-14
          if not mask.any(): break
          rem=diff/(2.0*mask.sum())
          h[first[mask]]+=rem; h[n-1-first[mask]]+=rem
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```

def coeff_pair(h,p,q,idx,M,off):
    L=np.zeros(M); Q=np.zeros(M)
    L[off+p-idx] += 1.0 - h[idx]
    L[off+idx-p] += -h[idx]
    L[off+q-idx] += 1.0 - h[idx]
    L[off+idx-q] += -h[idx]

def make_fourier_base(n,R):
  x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
  base=np.empty((R,n))
  for r in range(R): base[r]=np.cos(r*np.pi*(x-1.0))
  return base

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def project_feasible(h, n=None):
    if n is None:
        n = len(h)
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-15)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.where(h > 1e-15)[0]
        if idx.size:
            add = diff / min(idx.size, 100)
            h[:0]? h[idx] += add
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
            diff2 = target - h.sum()
            if abs(diff2) > 1e-14 and idx.size:
                j = idx[0]
                h[j] = np.clip(h[j] + diff2, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
The tail `h[idx] += add` may push outside; clip. If idx.size large and add small. Good.

`project_sym` as above. Need use `project_feasible` first, then sym, pair adjust. Pair adjust maybe after first sym enough. If target diff remains, loop.

```
def project_sym(h):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    h = 0.5*(h + h[::-1])
    first = np.arange(n//2)
    target = 0.5*n
    for _ in range(100):
       diff = target - h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       vals = 0.5*(h[first] + h[n-1-first])
       if diff>0:
          room = 1 - vals
          mask = room > 1e-14
          if not mask.any(): break
          add = diff/(2*mask.sum())
          h[first[mask]] += add
          h[n-1-first[mask]] += add
       else:
          mass = vals
          mask = mass > 1e-14
          if not mask.any(): break
          add = diff/(2*mask.sum())
          h[first[mask]] += add
          h[n-1-first[mask]] += add
       h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
This adjusts based on vals; if add bigger than room for some, clip then loop. If vals uniform, fine.

`score` straightforward.

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h, n_new):
    n=len(h)
    if n==n_new: return h.copy()
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    xnew=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
    return np.interp(xnew,x,h)
```

`check_feasible`: We'll define with parameters. Use constants? Top-level constants maybe `FEATOL = 1e-12`.
```
def check_feasible(gamma,c,L,pos,neg,zero,qinfo):
    if zero.any():
       # zero_cmax passed? We can pass zero_max. Avoid c[zero].max each time by parameter.
```
To optimize, pass `zero_max` instead of zero_mask. But check needs zero only for lower bound; if gamma < zero_max return false. No need mask. In solve, compute `zero_max = c[~(pos|neg)].max()` if any else 0.
```
def check_feasible(gamma, zero_max, c, L, pos, neg, qinfo):
    if gamma < zero_max - 1e-12: return False, None
    xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0
    if pos.any():
        ub = (gamma - c[pos]) / L[pos]
        xhi = min(xhi, float(np.min(ub)))
    if neg.any():
        lb = (gamma - c[neg]) / L[neg]
        xlo = max(xlo, float(np.max(lb)))
    if xlo < 0: xlo=0.0
    if xhi > 1: xhi=1.0
    if xlo > xhi + 1e-10: return False,None
    intervals=[(xlo,xhi)]
    for q,b,cc in qinfo:
        disc = b*b - 4.0*q*(gamma - cc)
        if disc > 1e-12:
            sd=np.sqrt(disc)
            r1=(-b-sd)/(2.0*q)
            r2=(-b+sd)/(2.0*q)
            f1=max(0.0, r1 + 1e-12)
            f2=min(1.0, r2 - 1e-12)
            if f1 < f2:
                new=[]
                for s,e in intervals:
                    if e <= f1 or s >= f2:
                        new.append((s,e))
                    else:
                        if s < f1:
                            new.append((s,f1))
                        if f2 < e:
                            new.append((f2,e))
                if not new: return False,None
                intervals=new
    # clean
    cleaned=[]
    for s,e in intervals:
       if e < s: s,e=e,s
       s=max(0.0,s); e=min(1.0,e)
       if e >= s - 1e-12:
          cleaned.append((s,e))
    if not cleaned: return False,None
    return True,cleaned
```

`choose_x`:
```
def choose_x(intervals,a):
    best=None; bestd=1e100
    for s,e in intervals:
        s=min(max(s,0.0),1.0); e=min(max(e,0.0),1.0)
        if s > e:
            if e < s: s,e=e,s
        if s - 1e-12 <= a <= e + 1e-12:
            return a
        if a < s:
            d=s-a; cand=s
        elif a > e:
            d=a-e; cand=e
        else:
            return a
        if d < bestd:
            bestd=d; best=cand
    if best is None: return a
    return best
```

`solve_x`:
```
def solve_x(c,L,Q,a):
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0: return a
    pos=L>1e-12; neg=L<-1e-12
    zero=~(pos|neg)
    if zero.any(): zero_max=float(c[zero].max())
    else: zero_max=0.0
    lo=zero_max
    if lo<0: lo=0.0
    hi=cmax
    if lo >= hi - 1e-12: return a
    qidx=np.nonzero(Q>1e-12)[0]
    qinfo=[]
    for t in qidx:
       q=Q[t]; b=L[t]+q; cc=c[t]-L[t]*a - q*a*(1.0-a)
       qinfo.append((float(q),float(b),float(cc)))
    # hi feasibility quick check? If not, return a?
    ok,_=check_feasible(hi,zero_max,c,L,pos,neg,qinfo)
    if not ok:
       return a
    for _ in range(35):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       ok,_=check_feasible(mid,zero_max,c,L,pos,neg,qinfo)
       if ok: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    if hi >= hi? no.
    ok,intervals=check_feasible(hi,zero_max,c,L,pos,neg,qinfo)
    if not ok:
       ok,intervals=check_feasible(cmax,zero_max,c,L,pos,neg,qinfo)
       if not ok: return a
    x=choose_x(intervals,a)
    if abs(x-a) < 1e-13: return a
    return x
```
If zero_max > cmax due numeric? return a. Good.

Could we set `lo = max(zero_max, cmax - 10)` to speed? Not safe? The minimal max may be less than cmax. But lower bound from sum? For c_m sum fixed? Not with sum drift. Zero is enough. If cmax large and optimum near cmax, 35 bisection range cmax. fine.

`coordinate_descent` code details:
```
def coordinate_descent(h,n,mode,stop_until,rng):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    if n != len(h): h=h[:n]
    if n%2: n-=1; h=h[:n]
    if n < 2: return h,1.0
    if mode=='sym':
        h=project_sym(h)
    else:
        h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    bound=float(c.max())*2.0/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_bound=bound; best_c=c.copy()
    idx=np.arange(n); M=2*n-1; off=n-1
    stall=0
    passes=0
    while time.time() < stop_until - 2.0:
        c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
        cur_max=float(c.max())
        updates=0
        if mode=='sym':
            # ensure sym
            h=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
            order=rng.permutation(n//2)
            for gi in order:
                if time.time() >= stop_until - 2.0:
                    break
                p=gi; q=n-1-p
                # if values differ, average
                a=0.5*(h[p]+h[q])
                if abs(a-h[p])>1e-15:
                    h[p]=a; h[q]=a
                    # recompute c? small mismatch? To be safe, if did, recompute c? But within pass, adjusting before solving changes c. Since should not happen, ignore or recompute. If happens, recompute:
                    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'); cur_max=c.max()
                L,Q=coeff_pair(h,p,q,idx,M,off)
                x=solve_x(c,L,Q,a)
                if x is not None and abs(x-a)>1e-13:
                    delta=x-a; rel=x*(1-x)-a*(1-a)
                    change=L*delta + Q*rel
                    c += change
                    new_max=float(c.max())
                    if new_max <= cur_max + 1e-9:
                        h[p]=x; h[q]=x
                        cur_max=new_max
                        updates +=1
                        if updates % 64 == 0:
                            c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
                            cur_max=float(c.max())
                    else:
                        c -= change
        else:
            order=rng.permutation(n)
            for p in order:
                if time.time() >= stop_until - 2.0:
                    break
                a=h[p]
                L,Q=coeff_single(h,p,idx,M,off)
                x=solve_x(c,L,Q,a)
                if abs(x-a)>1e-13:
                    delta=x-a; rel=x*(1-x)-a*(1-a)
                    change=L*delta+Q*rel
                    c += change
                    new_max=float(c.max())
                    if new_max <= cur_max + 1e-9:
                        h[p]=x; cur_max=new_max; updates+=1
                        if updates%64==0: recompute
                    else: c -= change
        passes +=1
        if mode=='sym':
            h=project_sym(h)
        else:
            h=project_feasible(h,n)
        c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
        bound=float(c.max())*2.0/n
        if bound < best_bound - 1e-12:
            best_bound=bound; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
        else:
            stall +=1
            if bound > best_bound + 1e-8:
                h=best_h.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); # but c variable not stored? we can recompute
                c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
                bound=best_bound
            if stall > 25:
                break
    # If final h better? best_h already
    return best_h, best_bound
```
Need if we reset h to best_h, next pass starts best, but `c` variable not used after loop except next pass recomputes. Good. But `best_c` variable set to c.copy; if reset use best_h. Now, Fourier functions. Need no lambda.

```
def make_fourier_base(n,R):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    base=np.empty((R,n), dtype=float)
    for r in range(R):
        base[r]=np.cos(r*np.pi*(x-1.0))
    return base

def fourier_h(params,n,R,base):
    h=np.full(n,0.5)
    for r in range(R):
        h += params[r]*base[r]
    return project_sym(h)

def fourier_obj(params,n,R,base):
    h=fourier_h(params,n,R,base)
    return score(h)
```
`np.full`.

Choose_x with prefer:
```
def choose_point(intervals, prefer):
   best=None; bestd=1e100
   for s,e in intervals:
      if s <= prefer <= e: return max(s,min(e,prefer))
      if prefer < s: d=s-prefer; cand=s
      elif prefer > e: d=prefer-e; cand=e
      else: return prefer
      if d < bestd: ...
   return best if best is not None else prefer
```

`coeff_pair` in code:
```
def coeff_pair(h,p,q,idx,M,off):
    L=np.zeros(M)
    Q=np.zeros(M)
    L[off+p-idx] += 1.0 - h[idx]
    L[off+idx-p] += -h[idx]
    L[off+q-idx] += 1.0 - h[idx]
    L[off+idx-q] += -h[idx]
    hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]
    L[off] -= 1.0 - hp
    L[off] += hp
    Q[off] += 1.0
    L[off] -= 1.0 - hq
    L[off] += hq
    Q[off] += 1.0
    t1=off+p-q; t2=off+q-p
    L[t1] -= 1.0 - hq
    L[t2] += hq
    Q[t1] += 1.0
    L[t2] -= 1.0 - hp
    L[t1] += hp
    Q[t2] += 1.0
    return L,Q
```
For p/q, if t1==off? only p=q. no. If t1==t2? p-q=0. no. If p/q such that t1 or t2 out of bounds? p,q in [0,n-1], off ± (n-1) gives 0..2n-2, ok.

`coeff_single`:
```
def coeff_single(h,p,idx,M,off):
    L=np.zeros(M); Q=np.zeros(M)
    L[off+p-idx] += 1.0 - h[idx]
    L[off+idx-p] += -h[idx]
    hv=h[p]
    L[off] -= 1.0 - hv
    L[off] += hv
    Q[off] = 1.0
    return L,Q
```
Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def project_feasible(h, n=None):
    if n is None:
        n = len(h)
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-15)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.where(h > 1e-15)[0]
        if idx.size:
            add = diff / min(idx.size, 100)
            h[idx] += add
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
            diff2 = target - h.sum()
            if abs(diff2) > 1e-14:
                h[idx[0]] = np.clip(h[idx[0]] + diff2, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def project_sym(h):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    first = np.arange(n // 2)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(120):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        vals = 0.5 * (h[first] + h[n - 1 - first])
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = (1.0 - vals) > 1e-14
        else:
            mask = vals > 1e-14
        if not mask.any():
            break
        add = diff / (2.0 * float(mask.sum()))
        h[first[mask]] += add
        h[n - 1 - first[mask]] += add
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def looks_sym(h):
    return bool(np.max(np.abs(h - h[::-1])) < 1e-7)


def score(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full"))) * (2.0 / n)


def resample_h(h, n_new):
    n = len(h)
    if n == n_new:
        return h.copy()
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    y = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    return np.interp(y, x, h)


def coeff_single(h, p, idx, M, off):
    L = np.zeros(M)
    Q = np.zeros(M)
    L[off + p - idx] += 1.0 - h[idx]
    L[off + idx - p] += -h[idx]
    hv = h[p]
    L[off] -= 1.0 - hv
    L[off] += hv
    Q[off] = 1.0
    return L, Q


def coeff_pair(h, p, q, idx, M, off):
    L = np.zeros(M)
    Q = np.zeros(M)
    hp = h[p]
    hq = h[q]

    L[off + p - idx] += 1.0 - h[idx]
    L[off + idx - p] += -h[idx]
    L[off + q - idx] += 1.0 - h[idx]
    L[off + idx - q] += -h[idx]

    L[off] -= 1.0 - hp
    L[off] += hp
    Q[off] += 1.0

    L[off] -= 1.0 - hq
    L[off] += hq
    Q[off] += 1.0

    t1 = off + p - q
    t2 = off + q - p
    L[t1] -= 1.0 - hq
    L[t2] += hq
    Q[t1] += 1.0

    L[t2] -= 1.0 - hp
    L[t1] += hp
    Q[t2] += 1.0
    return L, Q


def choose_point(intervals, prefer):
    prefer = min(1.0, max(0.0, prefer))
    best = None
    bestd = 1e100
    for s, e in intervals:
        s = min(1.0, max(0.0, s))
        e = min(1.0, max(0.0, e))
        if e < s:
            s, e = e, s
        if s - 1e-12 <= prefer <= e + 1e-12:
            return max(s, min(e, prefer))
        if prefer < s:
            d = s - prefer
            cand = s
        elif prefer > e:
            d = prefer - e
            cand = e
        else:
            return prefer
        if d < bestd:
            bestd = d
            best = cand
    return best if best is not None else prefer


def check_feasible(gamma, zero_max, Lpos, cpos, Lneg, cneg, a, qinfo):
    if gamma < zero_max - 1e-12:
        return False, None
    xlo = 0.0
    xhi = 1.0
    if Lpos.size:
        ub = (gamma - (cpos - Lpos * a)) / Lpos
        xhi = min(xhi, float(np.min(ub)))
    if Lneg.size:
        lb = (gamma - (cneg - Lneg * a)) / Lneg
        xlo = max(xlo, float(np.max(lb)))
    xlo = max(0.0, xlo)
    xhi = min(1.0, xhi)
    if xlo > xhi + 1e-10:
        return False, None
    intervals = [(xlo, xhi)]
    for q, b, cc in qinfo:
        disc = b * b - 4.0 * q * (gamma - cc)
        if disc > 1e-12:
            sd = float(np.sqrt(disc))
            r1 = (b - sd) / (2.0 * q)
            r2 = (b + sd) / (2.0 * q)
            f1 = max(0.0, r1 + 1e-12)
            f2 = min(1.0, r2 - 1e-12)
            if f1 < f2:
                new = []
                for s, e in intervals:
                    if e <= f1 or s >= f2:
                        new.append((s, e))
                    else:
                        if s < f1:
                            new.append((s, f1))
                        if f2 < e:
                            new.append((f2, e))
                if not new:
                    return False, None
                intervals = new
    cleaned = []
    for s, e in intervals:
        if e < s:
            s, e = e, s
        s = min(1.0, max(0.0, s))
        e = min(1.0, max(0.0, e))
        if e >= s - 1e-12:
            cleaned.append((s, e))
    if not cleaned:
        return False, None
    return True, cleaned


def solve_x(c, L, Q, a, prefer):
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        return a
    qq = Q > 1e-12
    lpos = (L > 1e-12) & (~qq)
    lneg = (L < -1e-12) & (~qq)
    linz = (~qq) & ~(lpos | lneg)
    zero_max = float(c[linz].max()) if np.any(linz) else 0.0
    if zero_max >= cmax - 1e-12:
        return a
    Lpos = L[lpos]
    cpos = c[lpos]
    Lneg = L[lneg]
    cneg = c[lneg]
    qidx = np.nonzero(qq)[0]
    qinfo = []
    for t in qidx:
        q = float(Q[t])
        b = float(L[t] + q)
        cc = float(c[t] - L[t] * a - q * a * (1.0 - a))
        qinfo.append((q, b, cc))
    ok, _ = check_feasible(cmax, zero_max, Lpos, cpos, Lneg, cneg, a, qinfo)
    if not ok:
        return a
    lo = zero_max
    if lo < 0.0:
        lo = 0.0
    hi = cmax
    for _ in range(36):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        ok, _ = check_feasible(mid, zero_max, Lpos, cpos, Lneg, cneg, a, qinfo)
        if ok:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    ok, intervals = check_feasible(hi, zero_max, Lpos, cpos, Lneg, cneg, a, qinfo)
    if not ok:
        ok, intervals = check_feasible(cmax, zero_max, Lpos, cpos, Lneg, cneg, a, qinfo)
        if not ok:
            return a
    x = choose_point(intervals, prefer)
    return x

def coordinate_descent(h, n, mode, stop_until, rng):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if n is None:
        n = len(h)
    if n != len(h):
        h = h[:n]
    if n % 2:
        n -= 1
        h = h[:n]
    if n < 2:
        return h, 1.0
    if mode == "sym":
        h = project_sym(h)
    else:
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    bound = float(c.max()) * (2.0 / n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_bound = bound
    idx = np.arange(n)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    off = n - 1
    target_sum = 0.5 * n
    stall = 0

    while time.time() < stop_until - 2.0:
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        cur_max = float(c.max())
        cur_sum = float(h.sum())
        updates = 0

        if mode == "sym":
            h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
            order = rng.permutation(n // 2)
            for gi in order:
                if time.time() >= stop_until - 2.0:
                    break
                p = gi
                q = n - 1 - p
                a = 0.5 * (h[p] + h[q])
                if abs(a - h[p]) > 1e-14:
                    h[p] = a
                    h[q] = a
                    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
                    cur_max = float(c.max())
                    cur_sum = float(h.sum())
                prefer = a + 0.075 * (target_sum - cur_sum)
                L, Q = coeff_pair(h, p, q, idx, M, off)
                x = solve_x(c, L, Q, a, prefer)
                if abs(x - a) > 1e-13:
                    delta = x - a
                    rel = x * (1.0 - x) - a * (1.0 - a)
                    change = L * delta + Q * rel
                    c += change
                    new_max = float(c.max())
                    if new_max <= cur_max + 1e-9:
                        h[p] = x
                        h[q] = x
                        cur_max = new_max
                        cur_sum += 2.0 * delta
                        updates += 1
                        if updates % 64 == 0:
                            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
                            cur_max = float(c.max())
                            cur_sum = float(h.sum())
                    else:
                        c -= change
        else:
            order = rng.permutation(n)
            for p in order:
                if time.time() >= stop_until - 2.0:
                    break
                a = h[p]
                prefer = a + 0.15 * (target_sum - cur_sum)
                L, Q = coeff_single(h, p, idx, M, off)
                x = solve_x(c, L, Q, a, prefer)
                if abs(x - a) > 1e-13:
                    delta = x - a
                    rel = x * (1.0 - x) - a * (1.0 - a)
                    change = L * delta + Q * rel
                    c += change
                    new_max = float(c.max())
                    if new_max <= cur_max + 1e-9:
                        h[p] = x
                        cur_max = new_max
                        cur_sum += delta
                        updates += 1
                        if updates % 64 == 0:
                            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
                            cur_max = float(c.max())
                            cur_sum = float(h.sum())
                    else:
                        c -= change

        if mode == "sym":
            h = project_sym(h)
        else:
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        bound = float(c.max()) * (2.0 / n)
        if bound < best_bound - 1e-12:
            best_bound = bound
            best_h = h.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if bound > best_bound + 1e-8:
                h = best_h.copy()
                bound = best_bound
            if stall > 25:
                break
    return best_h, best_bound


def make_fourier_base(n, R):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    base = np.empty((R, n), dtype=float)
    for r in range(R):
        base[r] = np.cos(r * np.pi * (x - 1.0))
    return base


def fourier_h(params, n, R, base):
    h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    for r in range(R):
        h += params[r] * base[r]
    return project_sym(h)


def fourier_obj(params, n, R, base):
    h = fourier_h(params, n, R, base)
    return score(h)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception:
        h0 = np.clip(1.0 - np.abs(np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, 700) - 1.0), 0.0, 1.0)

    n0 = len(h0)
    if n0 % 2:
        n0 -= 1
        h0 = h0[:n0]
    if n0 < 100:
        h0 = resample_h(h0, 100)
        n0 = len(h0)

    h_full = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    h_sym = project_sym(h0)
    c_full = score(h_full)
    c_sym = score(h_sym)

    best_h = h_sym if c_sym < c_full else h_full
    best_c = min(c_sym, c_full)
    best_n = n0
    best_mode = "sym" if c_sym < c_full else "full"

    main_stop = max(t0 + 30.0, deadline - 550.0)
    h_main, c_main = coordinate_descent(best_h, best_n, best_mode, main_stop, rng)
    if c_main < best_c + 1e-12:
        if c_main < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h_main, c_main, len(h_main)
            best_mode = "sym" if looks_sym(best_h) else "full"

    other_stop = max(time.time(), deadline - 250.0)
    if time.time() < other_stop - 20.0:
        if best_mode == "full":
            h_oth, c_oth = coordinate_descent(project_sym(best_h), best_n, "sym", other_stop, rng)
        else:
            h_oth, c_oth = coordinate_descent(project_feasible(best_h, best_n), best_n, "full", other_stop, rng)
        if c_oth < best_c + 1e-12 and c_oth < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h_oth, c_oth, len(h_oth)
            best_mode = "sym" if looks_sym(best_h) else "full"

    if time.time() < deadline - 130.0:
        R = 4
        nF = best_n
        if nF % 4:
            nF = max(128, (nF // 4) * 4)
        if nF > 720:
            nF = 704
        base = make_fourier_base(nF, R)
        for _ in range(2):
            if time.time() >= deadline - 70.0:
                break
            x0 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, R)
            try:
                res = minimize(fourier_obj, x0, args=(nF, R, base), method="Nelder-Mead",
                               options={"maxiter": 60, "xatol": 1e-6, "fatol": 1e-9})
                hF = fourier_h(res.x, nF, R, base)
                stop = min(deadline - 50.0, time.time() + 25.0)
                hF2, cF = coordinate_descent(hF, nF, "sym", stop, rng)
                if cF < best_c + 1e-12 and cF < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = hF2, cF, len(hF2)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
        for n_try in (350, 450, 650):
            if time.time() >= deadline - 35.0:
                break
            if n_try == best_n:
                continue
            h_int = project_sym(resample_h(best_h, n_try))
            stop = min(deadline - 30.0, time.time() + 18.0)
            hn, cn = coordinate_descent(h_int, n_try, "sym", stop, rng)
            if cn < best_c + 1e-12 and cn < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hn, cn, n_try

    for sig in (0.01, 0.03, 0.08):
        if time.time() >= deadline - 25.0:
            break
        hp = project_sym(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, sig, best_n))
        stop = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + 12.0)
        hn, cn = coordinate_descent(hp, best_n, "sym", stop, rng)
        if cn < best_c + 1e-12 and cn < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hn, cn

    final_mode = "sym" if looks_sym(best_h) else "full"
    if time.time() < deadline - 4.0:
        hn, cn = coordinate_descent(best_h, best_n, final_mode, deadline - 3.0, rng)
        if cn < best_c + 1e-12 and cn < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hn, cn
        if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
            other = "full" if final_mode == "sym" else "sym"
            hn2, cn2 = coordinate_descent(best_h, best_n, other, deadline - 1.5, rng)
            if cn2 < best_c + 1e-12 and cn2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hn2, cn2

    saved_h = best_h.copy()
    saved_c = best_c
    if looks_sym(best_h):
        hproj = project_sym(best_h)
    else:
        hproj = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    cproj = score(hproj)
    if cproj <= saved_c + 1e-12:
        best_h, best_c = hproj, cproj
    else:
        best_h, best_c = saved_h, saved_c

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(len(best_h))
```
